# Coverage list

Generated by `python3 tools/check_coverage.py --write`; `lint-coverage` (tools/check_all.sh) fails when it is
not the list of today's code. What each column means: the head of tools/check_coverage.py and
docs/dev/TESTING.md "Coverage list". The **spec** column: a spec that loads the file and names the function,
`via <spec>` (loaded and run through its callers), `world` (only the whole-server or whole-client worlds), a
web walk, or `none`. "What it must do" is the first sentence of the comment above the function (`-` when it
has none: its file's head comment in docs/dev/FILE_NOTES.md says what the file does).

## Totals

| | functions |
|---|---:|
| named by a spec | 1128 |
| via | 5741 |
| world | 148 |
| walk | 388 |
| none | 275 |
| all | 7680 |

Client entry points: 110, 88 driven by a spec that loads their file, 22 known gaps (tools/coverage_gaps.txt).
